For each run, normalize to sum=1, minimize 2n max c using multiplicative update. We can implement in Python with numpy FFT. Need ensure final exact score using evaluate_sequence maybe many times? We can score locally fast approximate, then exact for best.

Multiplicative update details:
Let a length n nonnegative, normalize sum=1. Compute c=convolve_full(a,a) via FFT clamp. Let cmax = max c. Score=2n cmax. (since sum1). Choose a set of indices K where c_k > θ cmax (top T maybe 16 or 64). Compute D = convolution derivative: D_i = (1/|K|)∑_{k∈K} a_{k-i} where 0≤k-i<n. This can be computed by creating P length L zeros with 1/|K| at K, then D = cross_correlate(P, a): D_i = ∑_k P_k a_{k-i}. This is exactly np.convolve(P_rev, a)? Let's derive with FFT: We want for each i, sum over k of P[k] * a[k-i] if valid. Define B = P[::-1] maybe. Linear convolution a * P_rev: (a * P_rev)_i = ∑_j a_j P_rev_{i-j}=∑_j a_j P_{?} P_rev_m=P[ L-1-m]? Let's just compute directly if T small via loops? For n=2048, T up to64, direct O(Tn)=131k, fine. D_i = sum_{k} P[k]*a[k-i]; for each k, add P[k]*a[0:k+1] to D[0:n? actually k-i>=0 => i≤k]. So D[0:k+1] += P[k]*a[:k+1]. For k up to2n-2. O(Tn) simple. If T=64,n=4096 ->262k per iter okay. Could also FFT.

Update: a_new = a * exp(-eta * D) then normalize. But D has scale about #top*avg a? For sum1, D_i ~ O(1/n). Need eta adaptive. Could use line search: choose eta from [1,2,4,...] maybe. However if D_i large for variables involved in high coeffs, they decrease, others maybe unchanged. Since sum normalization may increase low variables? Multiplicative update on log a. Given the complexity, a generic optimizer over full sequence of length maybe 500-2000 could discover a good pattern. Let's make it as robust as possible.

We can use an evolutionary strategy over vectors:
- Population of candidates (maybe 20-50) with fast scores.
- Generate offspring by:
   * Block scale/perturb.
   * Convex combinations of parents.
   * LP-like update from parent (approx primal-dual).
   * Random local flips (if sparse, support random).
- Keep best.

But random local search may be slow. Use local gradient for individual.

Let's implement fast_conv via FFT robust:
```
def _fft_len(n): return 1 << (int(n)-1).bit_length()

def fast_convolve(a):
    n=len(a); L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c
```
Use float64. For n large >16384, FFT memory maybe. Choose max N=2048/4096. We can include length 8192? L=262144, fa ~128k complex ~2MB, okay; convolve 0.5ms? okay. But local slower. Use max N=4096 maybe.

fast_score(a):
```
s=sum(a)
if s<=1e-12: return inf
c=fast_convolve(a)
return 2*len(a)*c.max()/ (s*s)
```

Implement smooth_optimize:
```
def smooth_optimize(a, max_iter, beta, lr, deadline, rng, tag):
    n=len(a)
    a=normalize_seq(a)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    best=z.copy(); best_a=a.copy()
    best_sc=fast_score(a)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    while max_iter>0 and time.monotonic()<deadline:
        z -= z.mean()
        ez=np.exp(z - np.max(z))
        a=ez/ez.sum()
        c=fast_convolve(a)
        maxc=c.max()
        sh=beta*(c-maxc)
        p=np.exp(sh); p_sum=p.sum(); p/=p_sum
        D=correlation_pm(a,p,n)
        grad_a=(2.0*n/beta)*D
        grad_z=a*(grad_a - np.dot(grad_a,a))
        # optional rescale gradient
        g=grad_z
        t+=1
        m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*g
        v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*g*g
        mhat=m/(1-beta1**t); vhat=v/(1-beta2**t)
        z = z - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
        # if z nonfinite break
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): z=best; break
        a2=softmax(z)
        sc2=fast_score(a2)
        if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=sc2; best=z.copy(); best_a=a2.copy()
        # maybe if no improvement for 200 iterations, restart logits from best + noise? 
    return best_a,best_sc
```
But using fixed z update without line search may drift. We can keep best and if worst, revert z=best? Maybe if score increased, continuing from worse z might not recover. Better use line search / revert:
   z_candidate = z - step
   compute score; if score < current_score? But current maybe not improving surrogate; we can accept if not much worse or use adaptive lr. Simpler: Do gradient descent on smoothed surrogate but track best; still use current z (not revert) so can pass hills. To avoid diverging, scale gradient by norm: `step_scale = lr / max(1.0, np.linalg.norm(g))`? Adam already per-coordinate; if global norm huge, maybe. We can normalize `g = g / np.linalg.norm(g)` then use lr constant 0.05? This makes direction only. For N=1024, step in z small. Let's do both: compute grad; if 2-norm >0, `step = lr * grad / max(1.0, norm)`? That may slow. Use Adam with base lr=0.05. Ada handles scale.
   Also after each step, if current global score increased by >0.1, set z=best (restart from best) and m=v=0? Could help. Use stagnation: if no improvement for 100 iter, z = best + 0.02*randn? But careful.

Maybe more stable: Use `z_new = z`; perform a line search in direction `d = -grad_z`:
   current_a, current_sc computed. For factor in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05], z_test = z - factor*lr*grad_z (with maybe norm scaling), score. Sc=...; choose best factor if score<current_sc or factor smallest if all worse? This is backtracking. But grad_z can have components; direction normalized. Let's implement gradient descent with momentum and line search carefully:

```
def block_refine(a, deadline, rng, max_iter, base_temp):
   n=len(a); a=normalize; best=a; best_sc=fast_score
   temp=0.05
   for it...
      cand=a.copy()
      op=rng.random()
      if op<0.5: block scale: width=rng.integers(...); start; cand *= exp(rng.normal(0,sigma, width)); norm
      elif op<0.7: two-block swap? cut length, reverse segment
      elif op<0.8: kernel blur: cid = cand * kernel? Could smooth
      else: mix with best: cand = (1-t)a + t best
      score=fast_score
      if score < best_sc: update; temp*=0.95
      elif score < best_sc*(1+temp): accept (anneal)
   return best,best_sc
```

Let's write helper functions carefully.

Softmax helper:
```
def softmax_v(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): 
       return np.full_like(z,1.0/len(z))
    return e/s
```

correlation_pm:
```
def correlation_pm(a,p,n):
    # p length 2n-1, a length n
    L = 1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()
    A = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64); A[:n]=a
    # P_rev length 2n-1
    P = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64); P[:len(p)] = p[::-1]
    FA=np.fft.rfft(A); FP=np.fft.rfft(P)
    C=np.fft.irfft(FA*FP, L)
    m=len(p)
    D=C[m-1 : m-1-n : -1]
    # D length n? Need slice stop exclusive. Use list? C[m-1 : m-1-n-?]. Let's define D = np.empty(n); D[:] = C[m-1 : -1 if ...]. Simpler reverse:
    D = C[m-1 : m-n-? Need exact.
```
For N=2,m=3: want C[2], C[1]. Python slice C[2:0:-1] -> [C2,C1]. general `C[m-1 : m-1-n : -1]` yields stop before index m-1-n? Example n=3,m=5: want C4,C3,C2. Slice 4:1:-1 -> [4,3,2]. Good. For n=2,m=3: 2:0:-1 -> [2,1]. Good. Use that. Ensure length n. Return np.maximum(D,0?) D may tiny neg due FFT; clip to 0 because p,a nonneg.

Convolve helper:
```
def fast_convolve(a):
  n=a.size; L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1]
  np.maximum(c,0,out=c); return c
```
If n=0 handle.

```
def fast_score_norm(a):
  n=a.size; if n==0 return inf
  c=fast_convolve(a); return 2.0*n*c.max()
```

normalize:
```
def normalize_pos(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
  a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0); a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
  s=a.sum()
  if s<=1e-12:
      a[:] = 1.0/len(a); return a
  a/=s
  return a
```

```
def thue_morse_pos(n):
```

```
def make_inits(n, rng):
  inits=[]
  inits.append(np.ones(n))
  inits.append(np.linspace(1, n, n))
  inits.append(rev)
  for r in [0.8,0.9,0.95,0.99, max(0.5,1-2/n)]:
     inits.append(r**np.arange(n)); inits.append(rev)
  for p in [0.5,1,2]: inits.append((np.arange(n)+1)**p); rev
  # sinusoidal
  for freq in [1,2,3]:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      inits.append(0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x))
      inits.append(0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x))
  # thue if n power of 2
  # random
  for sigma in [0.5,1.0,2.0]: inits.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n)))
  # sparse random: choose support k ~ n/4? positive zeros
  for k in [max(2,n//16), max(2,n//4)]:
      a=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,k,replace=False); a[idx]=rng.uniform(0.1,1,k); inits.append(a)
  return inits
```

Let's implement smooth_optimize with line search and restarts top-level.

```
def smooth_optimize(a, max_iter, beta, lr, deadline, rng, tag):
    n=len(a); a=normalize_pos(a)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    best_z=z.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=fast_score_norm(a)
    cur_z=z.copy(); cur_a=a; cur_sc=best_sc
    stagn=0; best_iters=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       cur_a = softmax_v(cur_z)
       c=fast_convolve(cur_a)
       maxc=c.max()
       if not finite: best_sc=cur_sc; break
       shifted=beta*(c-maxc); p=np.exp(shifted); s=p.sum(); if s==0: p=c/c.sum()? else p/=s
       D=correlation_pm(cur_a,p,n)
       grad_a=(2.0*n/beta)*D
       grad=cur_a*(grad_a - np.dot(grad_a,cur_a))
       # center grad? Large constant removal already by formula.
       gnorm=np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad))
       if not finite or gnorm<1e-14:
           stagn+=1
       else:
           d=-grad/gnorm
           # base step: we can use lr * scale, scale maybe 0.5? 
           improved_step=False
           for fac in (1.0, 0.6, 0.35, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05):
                step=lr*fac
                z_test=cur_z + step*d
                z_test -= z_test.mean()
                a_test=softmax_v(z_test)
                sc=fast_score_norm(a_test)
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc=sc; best_a=a_test.copy(); best_z=z_test.copy(); best_iters=it
                if sc < cur_sc - 1e-12:
                    cur_z=z_test; cur_a=a_test; cur_sc=sc; stagn=0; improved_step=True; break
           if not improved_step:
                # If no descent in max, try surrogate by moving small in smoothed direction? Use fac=0.02 maybe always accept to move?
                step=lr*0.05
                z_test=cur_z + step*d; z_test-=z_test.mean()
                cur_z=z_test
                stagn+=1
       if it%100==0: print(f"{tag} it {it} best {best_sc:.6f} cur {cur_sc:.6f}")
       # Stagnation: reset to best with noise
       if stagn>80:
           cur_z = best_z + 0.05*float(n)**-0.5 * rng.normal(size=n)
           cur_z -= cur_z.mean()
           cur_a=softmax_v(cur_z); cur_sc=fast_score_norm(cur_a)
           stagn=0
    # maybe final from best_z
    return best_a,best_sc
```
But this line search requires computing cur_a at start each iter; inside factors computes a_test and score. We also need current c for gradient; if no improvement, we still computed. Block refine implementation:
```
def block_refine(a, max_iter, deadline, rng, tag):
    n=len(a); a=normalize_pos(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=fast_score_norm(a); cur=a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc; temp=0.1
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time>deadline: break
       cand=cur.copy()
       op=rng.random()
       if op<0.4:
          width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//8)))
          start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width)); stop=start+width
          factor=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, stop-start))
          cand[start:stop]*=factor
       elif op<0.6:
          # unscale (dividing)
          width...; factor=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.2,width)); cand[start:stop]/=np.maximum(factor,1e-6)
       elif op<0.75:
          # swap two random blocks
          L1,L2 random nonoverlap...
       elif op<0.85:
          # blur with small kernel: use np.convolve(cand,np.array([0.25,0.5,0.25]),'same')? keep nonneg, maybe move mass; normalize
          k=np.array([0.2,0.6,0.2]); cand=np.convolve(cand,k,mode='same'); np.maximum(cand,0)
       else:
          t=0.1+0.3*rng.random(); cand=(1-t)*cur + t*best
       cand=normalize_pos(cand)
       sc=fast_score_norm(cand)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best=cand.copy(); cur=cand.copy(); temp=max(0.02, temp*0.97)
       elif sc < best_sc*(1+temp*0.1): # small
          cur=cand.copy()
       # occasional exact? no
    return best,best_sc
```
Normalize after divide can blow zeros; okay.

Maybe block refine from uniform can break symmetry; but random perturb maybe.

Helper is_prime_number:
```
def is_prime_number(x):
    if x<2: return False
    if x%2==0: return x==2
    d=3
    while d*d<=x:
       if x%d==0: return False
       d+=2
    return True
```

```
def make_legendre(n_target, rng):
   p=next_prime_at_least(max(17,n_target))
   if p > 3*n_target or p>5000: return None
   chi=np.ones(p)
   for i in range(1,p):
      if pow(i,p-2,p)==1:
         chi[i]=1.0
      else:
         chi[i]=-1.0
   # choose orientation maybe two versions
   return 0.5*(1+chi), 0.5*(1-chi)
```

```
def make_sinusoid(n,freq,phase):

 val=np.maximum(val,1e-3)
```

```
def make_geo(n, r, rev=False):
```

```
def lp_projection(f, deadline, rng):
  n=f.size
  if n>600: return f, fast_score_norm(f)
  try: import scipy.optimize as opt
  except: return
  best=f; best_sc=fast_score_norm(f)
  for it in range(3):
    if time>deadline break
    c=fast_convolve(best); M=c.max()*1.001
    # build A; maybe if n>400 use scipy.sparse? Dense memory 2*600*600=720k fine.
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
    for k in range(2*n-1): ...
    try:
       res=opt.linprog(-np.ones(n), A=A, b=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': max(1, int(deadline-time.monotonic()-5))})
    except: break
    if not res.success: break
    g=normalize_pos(np.maximum(res.x,0))
    # try blends
    for t in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]:
       cand=normalize_pos((1-t)*best + t*g)
       sc=fast_score_norm(cand)
       if sc<best_sc: best=cand; best_sc=sc
  return best,best_sc
```

Implement `next_pow2(x)`:
```
def next_pow2(x):
   if x<=1: return 1
   return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
```

Use in helpers.

`fast_convolve`: if n==0 return empty. For n=1, L=1, fa=rfft(a,1), c=irfft(fa*fa,1)[:1]. Works.

`correlation_pm`: If n==1, return np.array([p[0]*a[0]]). Else L=next_pow2(3*n-2). A zeros length L; A[:n]=a. P zeros; P[:m]=p[::-1]. C=irfft... D= C[m-1:m-n-1:-1]. Since p length m. Return np.clip(D,0,None).

`make_inits`: Need not include too many for large n; limit. Use a list `out`; we can generate deterministic.
```
def make_inits(n, rng, max_count):
  out=[]
  def add(a):
     a=np.asarray(a,float); if a.size != n: return; if finite and sum>0: out.append(normalize_pos(a))
  add(np.ones(n))
  x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)+1
  for p in [1.0, 0.5, 2.0]:
      add(x**p); add((x**p)[::-1])
      if len(out) < max_count: add(rng.permutation(x**p))
  for r in [0.8,0.9,0.95,0.98]:
      add(r**np.arange(n)); add((r**np.arange(n))[::-1])
  for f in [1,2,3,5]:
      t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
      add(0.2+0.8*np.abs(np.sin(np.pi*f*t)))
      add(0.2+0.8*np.abs(np.cos(np.pi*f*t)))
  # thue if power
  if n & (n-1)==0:
      th=make_thue(n); if th is not None: add(th)
  # random
  add(np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,n)))
  add(np.abs(rng.normal(0,1,n))+0.1)
  # sparse random
  for frac in (0.25,0.5):
      k=max(1,int(n*frac)); a=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,k,replace=False); a[idx]=rng.uniform(0.1,1.0,k); add(a)
  # legendre if n near prime? maybe if max_count and n<=2048
  leg=make_legendre(n); if leg: add both
  # trim
  return out[:max_count]
```

```python
"""
Search for low-score nonnegative sequences.

The program combines:
- deterministic/parametric initial guesses (ramps, geometric, trigonometric,
  quadratic-phase, Thue-Morse, random, sparse),
- full-vector smoothed projected-gradient optimization of
  2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)**2),
- local block/refinement moves,
- an optional LP projection step (when scipy is available),
- final exact verification with the provided evaluate_sequence().

All helper functions are top-level and no lambdas/closures are used.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_pos(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a.fill(1.0 / a.size)
        return a
    a /= s
    return a


def softmax_v(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        e = np.ones_like(z)
        s = float(e.sum())
    return e / s


def fast_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def fast_score_norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def exact_score_list(lst):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if val is None:
        return float("inf")
    val = float(val)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return float("inf")
    return val


def correlation_pm(a, p, n):
    """
    D_i = sum_k p_k * a_{k-i}, with a_j=0 outside [0,n-1].
    a: length n, p: length 2n-1, both nonnegative.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if int(n) == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if int(n) == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * p[0]) if p.size else 0.0], dtype=np.float64)

    L = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    A = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    A[:n] = a

    P = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    P[:p.size] = p[::-1]

    FA = np.fft.rfft(A)
    FP = np.fft.rfft(P)
    C = np.fft.irfft(FA * FP, L)
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    C[~np.isfinite(C)] = 0.0

    m = int(p.size)
    D = C[m - 1:m - n - 1:-1]
    if D.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        take = min(n, D.size)
        tmp[:take] = D[:take]
        D = tmp
    np.maximum(D, 0.0, out=D)
    return D


def smooth_optimize(a, max_iter, beta, lr, deadline, rng, tag):
    """
    Smoothed log-sum-exp approximating max convolution coefficient,
    optimized in log-softmax coordinates.
    """
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))

    best_z = z.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = fast_score_norm(best_a)

    cur_z = z.copy()
    cur_a = softmax_v(cur_z)
    cur_sc = fast_score_norm(cur_a)

    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    stagnation = 0
    best_iter = 0

    while max_iter > 0 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        max_iter -= 1
        t += 1
        cur_a = softmax_v(cur_z)
        c = fast_convolve(cur_a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        maxc = float(c.max())
        if maxc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxc):
            break

        shifted = beta * (c - maxc)
        p = np.exp(shifted)
        psum = float(p.sum())
        if psum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(psum):
            p = c / max(1e-300, float(c.sum()))
        else:
            p /= psum

        D = correlation_pm(cur_a, p, n)
        grad_a = (2.0 * n / float(beta)) * D
        gm = float(np.dot(grad_a, cur_a))
        grad = cur_a * (grad_a - gm)
        grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0

        gnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(grad * grad)))
        if gnorm < 1e-14:
            m = beta1 * m
            v = beta2 * v
            stagnation += 1
            if stagnation > 60:
                cur_z = best_z + (0.05 / float(np.sqrt(max(1, n)))) * rng.normal(size=n)
                cur_z -= float(np.mean(cur_z))
                cur_a = softmax_v(cur_z)
                cur_sc = fast_score_norm(cur_a)
                stagnation = 0
            continue

        d = -grad / gnorm

        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * d
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (d * d)
        b1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** t
        b2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** t
        step = lr * m / (np.sqrt(v / b2) + eps) / b1

        improved = False
        for fac in (1.0, 0.6, 0.35, 0.18, 0.08, 0.03):
            zt = cur_z + (fac * float(step).max() if False else 0.0)  # placeholder removed below
            # Do actual step using adaptive step but normalized to keep scale sane.
            step_vec = (fac * lr) * (m / (np.sqrt(v / b2) + eps) / b1)
            step_norm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(step_vec * step_vec)))
            if step_norm > 0.0:
                step_vec = step_vec * (0.5 / step_norm)
            zt = cur_z + step_vec
            zt -= float(np.mean(zt))
            at = softmax_v(zt)
            sc = fast_score_norm(at)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = at.copy()
                best_z = zt.copy()
                best_iter = t
            if sc < cur_sc - 1e-12:
                cur_z = zt
                cur_a = at
                cur_sc = sc
                stagnation = 0
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            # Small exploratory move in the smoothed-gradient direction.
            zt = cur_z - (0.03 / max(1.0, gnorm)) * grad
            zt -= float(np.mean(zt))
            cur_a = softmax_v(zt)
            cur_z = zt
            cur_sc = fast_score_norm(cur_a)
            stagnation += 1

        if stagnation > 120:
            cur_z = best_z + (0.08 / float(np.sqrt(max(1, n)))) * rng.normal(size=n)
            cur_z -= float(np.mean(cur_z))
            cur_a = softmax_v(cur_z)
            cur_sc = fast_score_norm(cur_a)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            stagnation = 0

    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        z_best_norm = best_z - float(np.mean(best_z))
        a_best = softmax_v(z_best_norm)
        sc_best = fast_score_norm(a_best)
        if sc_best < best_sc:
            best_a = a_best
            best_sc = sc_best

    return best_a, best_sc


def block_refine(a, deadline, rng, tag):
    """
    Fast local search using block scaling, reversal, smoothing, and blending.
    All scoring is via FFT; exact verification is done later.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    cur = normalize_pos(a)
    cur_sc = fast_score_norm(cur)
    best = cur.copy()
    best_sc = cur_sc
    temp = 0.05
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        cand = cur.copy()
        op = float(rng.random())

        if op < 0.40:
            lo = max(1, n // 300)
            hi = max(lo + 1, n // 4)
            width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.18, stop - start))
        elif op < 0.55:
            lo = max(1, n // 300)
            hi = max(lo + 1, n // 4)
            width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            factor = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, stop - start))
            factor = np.maximum(factor, 1e-6)
            cand[start:stop] /= factor
        elif op < 0.70:
            l = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - 1)))
            r = int(rng.integers(l + 1, n))
            cand[l:r] = cand[l:r][::-1]
        elif op < 0.85:
            k = np.array([0.25, 0.5, 0.25], dtype=np.float64)
            blurred = np.convolve(cand, k, mode="same")
            cand = 0.6 * cand + 0.4 * blurred
        else:
            t = float(0.1 + 0.4 * rng.random())
            cand = (1.0 - t) * cur + t * best

        cand = normalize_pos(cand)
        sc = fast_score_norm(cand)

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best = cand.copy()
            cur = cand.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            temp = max(0.004, temp * 0.96)
            if tag and (it % 2000 == 0):
                print(f"{tag} block best {best_sc:.6f}")
        elif sc < best_sc * (1.0 + 0.08 * temp):
            cur = cand.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 1500:
            cur = best + (0.04 / float(np.sqrt(max(1, n)))) * rng.normal(size=n)
            cur = normalize_pos(cur)
            no_improve = 0
            temp = max(temp, 0.03)

    return best, best_sc


def is_prime_number(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x < 2:
        return False
    if x % 2 == 0:
        return x == 2
    d = 3
    while d * d <= x:
        if x % d == 0:
            return False
        d += 2
    return True


def next_prime_at_least(x):
    p = max(3, int(x))
    if p % 2 == 0:
        p += 1
    while not is_prime_number(p):
        p += 2
    return p


def make_thue(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1 or (n & (n - 1)) != 0:
        return None
    t = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    for i in range(1, n):
        t[i] = t[i >> 1] ^ (i & 1)
    a = (t == 0).astype(np.float64)
    a += 1e-5
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    return a


def make_legendre_prime(target_n):
    n = int(target_n)
    if n < 17 or not is_prime_number(n):
        return None
    chi = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(1, n):
        chi[i] = 1.0 if pow(i, n - 2, n) == 1 else -1.0
    a1 = 0.5 * (1.0 + chi) + 1e-5
    a2 = 0.5 * (1.0 - chi) + 1e-5
    return [normalize_pos(a1), normalize_pos(a2)]


def make_inits(n, rng, max_count):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    out = []
    ar = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out.append(normalize_pos(a))

    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out.append(normalize_pos(a))

    a = ar + 1.0
    out.append(normalize_pos(a))
    out.append(normalize_pos(a[::-1]))

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        base = np.power(ar + 1.0, p)
        out.append(normalize_pos(base))
        out.append(normalize_pos(base[::-1]))
        if len(out) < max_count:
            out.append(normalize_pos(rng.permutation(base)))
        if len(out) >= max_count:
            break

    r = max(0.5, 1.0 - 2.0 / max(3.0, float(n)))
    for base_r in (0.8, 0.9, 0.95, 0.98, r):
        if base_r <= 0.0:
            continue
        base = np.power(base_r, ar)
        out.append(normalize_pos(base))
        out.append(normalize_pos(base[::-1]))
        if len(out) >= max_count:
            break

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    for freq in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
        out.append(normalize_pos(0.2 + 0.8 * np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * freq * x))))
        out.append(normalize_pos(0.2 + 0.8 * np.abs(np.cos(np.pi * freq * x))))
        if len(out) >= max_count:
            break

    if n <= 4096:
        q = np.mod(ar * ar, n).astype(np.float64)
        out.append(normalize_pos(0.2 + 0.8 * np.abs(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * q / max(1.0, float(n))))))
        out.append(normalize_pos(0.2 + 0.8 * np.abs(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * q / max(1.0, float(n))))))

    if (n & (n - 1)) == 0:
        th = make_thue(n)
        if th is not None:
            out.append(th)

    leg = make_legendre_prime(n)
    if leg:
        out.extend(leg)

    out.append(normalize_pos(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))))
    out.append(normalize_pos(np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)) + 0.05))

    for frac in (0.25, 0.5):
        k = max(1, int(frac * n))
        a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
        a[idx] = rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0, k)
        out.append(normalize_pos(a))

    cleaned = []
    for b in out:
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if b.size != n:
            continue
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
            continue
        cleaned.append(normalize_pos(b))
        if len(cleaned) >= max_count:
            break

    # Try to avoid exact duplicates by tiny perturbation if many occur.
    uniq = []
    for b in cleaned:
        uniq.append(b)
    return uniq


def shrink_candidates(candidates, max_keep):
    if len(candidates) <= max_keep:
        return candidates
    while len(candidates) > max_keep:
        worst_i = 0
        worst_s = -1.0
        for i, item in enumerate(candidates):
            s = item[0]
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s > worst_s:
                worst_s = s if np.isfinite(s) else s
                worst_i = i
        del candidates[worst_i]
    return candidates


def add_candidate(candidates, uid, seq, max_keep=60):
    arr = normalize_pos(seq)
    if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return uid
    sc = fast_score_norm(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return uid
    candidates.append((float(sc), int(uid), arr.copy()))
    uid += 1
    shrink_candidates(candidates, max_keep)
    return uid


def lp_projection(f, deadline):
    """
    Optional one-step variable projection:
    maximize sum(g) subject to f*g <= M, g >= 0.
    If scipy/highs is available, solve it; otherwise return unchanged.
    """
    f = normalize_pos(f)
    n = int(f.size)
    if n < 4 or n > 600:
        return f, fast_score_norm(f)

    try:
        from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except Exception:
        return f, fast_score_norm(f)

    best = f.copy()
    best_sc = fast_score_norm(best)
    f_work = f.copy()
    f_work = np.maximum(f_work, 1e-8 / float(n))
    f_work = normalize_pos(f_work)

    for it in range(3):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        c = fast_convolve(f_work)
        M = float(c.max()) * 1.001 + 1e-12
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(2 * n - 1):
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            A[k, lo:hi + 1] = f_work[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]

        try:
            timeout = max(1, int(deadline - time.monotonic() - 5))
            opts = {"time_limit": timeout}
            res = linprog(
                -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=(0, None),
                method="highs",
                options=opts,
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if not res.success or res.x is None:
            break
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
        np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
        g = normalize_pos(g)
        if g.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        for t in (0.2, 0.45, 0.7, 1.0):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * f_work + t * g
            cand = normalize_pos(cand + 1e-8 / float(n))
            sc = fast_score_norm(cand)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best = cand.copy()
        f_work = best.copy()
        f_work = np.maximum(f_work, 1e-8 / float(n))
        f_work = normalize_pos(f_work)

    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    hard_deadline = start_time + max(60.0, budget - 2.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    candidates = []
    uid = 0

    # Universal fallback: length-2 optimum and uniform.
    fallback2 = np.array([2.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
    uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, fallback2)
    uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))

    # Provided previous construction if present.
    parent_obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    parent = None
    if parent_obj is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(parent_obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p[~np.isfinite(p)] = 0.0
            np.maximum(p, 0.0, out=p)
            if p.size > 0 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = p
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, parent)
        noisy = parent.astype(np.float64).copy()
        noisy += 0.02 * max(1.0, np.sqrt(max(1.0, 1.0 / float(parent.size)))) * rng.normal(size=parent.size)
        uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, noisy)
        if parent.size <= 12000:
            uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, parent[::-1])

    # Phased generation and smoothing.
    # (length, max_iterations, per-run seconds, max initial guesses)
    configs = [
        (2, 20000, 1.0, 2),
        (4, 20000, 1.0, 3),
        (8, 16000, 2.0, 4),
        (16, 12000, 3.0, 5),
        (32, 10000, 4.0, 6),
        (64, 8000, 6.0, 6),
        (128, 6000, 8.0, 6),
        (256, 4000, 12.0, 5),
        (512, 2500, 16.0, 5),
        (1024, 1500, 22.0, 4),
        (2048, 900, 30.0, 3),
    ]

    smooth_phase_deadline = hard_deadline - 300.0
    for n, max_iter, run_s, max_inits in configs:
        if time.monotonic() >= smooth_phase_deadline:
            break
        if n > 4096:
            continue
        inits = make_inits(n, rng, max_inits)
        beta = max(20.0, 6.0 * float(n))
        lr = 0.35
        for idx, init in enumerate(inits):
            if time.monotonic() >= smooth_phase_deadline:
                break
            run_deadline = min(smooth_phase_deadline, time.monotonic() + max(0.2, run_s))
            tag = f"smooth n={n} init={idx}"
            try:
                best_a, sc = smooth_optimize(init, max_iter, beta, lr, run_deadline, rng, tag)
            except Exception:
                best_a, sc = init, fast_score_norm(normalize_pos(init))
            uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, best_a)
            uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, best_a[::-1])

    # If the parent is large, apply block refinement directly to it.
    if parent is not None and parent.size <= 12000:
        if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 180.0:
            run_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 150.0, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
            try:
                b, bsc = block_refine(parent, run_deadline, rng, "parent-block")
            except Exception:
                b, bsc = parent, fast_score_norm(parent)
            uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, b)
            uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, b[::-1])

    # Sort by fast score.
    candidates.sort()

    # Local refinement of best prompts.
    block_deadline = hard_deadline - 80.0
    top_count = min(8, len(candidates))
    block_budgets = [80.0, 65.0, 55.0, 45.0, 35.0, 25.0, 20.0, 15.0]
    for idx in range(top_count):
        if time.monotonic() >= block_deadline:
            break
        sc, _, seq = candidates[idx]
        budget = block_budgets[idx] if idx < len(block_budgets) else 15.0
        budget = min(budget, max(0.0, block_deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0))
        if budget <= 0.5:
            break
        try:
            b, bsc = block_refine(seq, time.monotonic() + budget, rng, f"block-top{idx}")
        except Exception:
            b, bsc = seq, sc
        uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, b)
        uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, b[::-1])
        candidates.sort()

    # Optional LP projection for small best candidates.
    if len(candidates) > 0 and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 120.0:
        for idx in range(min(2, len(candidates))):
            if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 100.0:
                break
            sc, _, seq = candidates[idx]
            if seq.size > 600:
                continue
            lp_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
            try:
                lp_best, lp_sc = lp_projection(seq, lp_deadline)
            except Exception:
                lp_best, lp_sc = seq, sc
            uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, lp_best)
            uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, lp_best[::-1])
        candidates.sort()

    # One final short block refinement on the very best prompt.
    if len(candidates) > 0 and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 40.0:
        final_budget = min(30.0, max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0))
        if final_budget > 1.0:
            sc, _, seq = candidates[0]
            try:
                b, bsc = block_refine(seq, time.monotonic() + final_budget, rng, "final-block")
            except Exception:
                b, bsc = seq, sc
            uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, b)
            uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, b[::-1])
            candidates.sort()

    # Ensure fallbacks are available.
    candidates.sort()
    uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, fallback2)
    uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))
    if parent is not None:
        uid = add_candidate(candidates, uid, parent)

    # Exact verification of the best candidates.
    exact_checked = 0
    best_exact_score = float("inf")
    best_exact_seq = fallback2.copy()

    # Always verify fallbacks first, in case all search outputs fail exact checks.
    fallback_exact = exact_score_list([float(x) for x in fallback2])
    if np.isfinite(fallback_exact):
        best_exact_score = fallback_exact
        best_exact_seq = fallback2.copy()

    for idx, item in enumerate(candidates):
        sc, _, seq = item
        n = int(seq.size)
        # Limit expensive exact evaluations for very long sequences.
        if n > 8000 and exact_checked >= 3:
            continue
        if n > 4000 and exact_checked >= 6:
            continue
        if idx > 18:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 1.0:
            break
        try:
            ex = exact_score_list([float(x) for x in seq])
        except Exception:
            ex = float("inf")
        exact_checked += 1
        if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = ex
            best_exact_seq = seq.copy()
        # If we found something below the target or close, keep checking a few more
        # unless time is tight.
        if best_exact_score < 1.55 and exact_checked >= 6:
            break

    return [float(x) for x in best_exact_seq]
```
